# 4. eDiscovery AI

## 4.1 Relativity aiR

# Competitive Research Report: Relativity / RelativityOne + aiR AI Family (as of mid-2026)

---

## 1. What It Is, Ownership, Scale, Leadership

Relativity (Chicago-based, formerly kCura) is the dominant eDiscovery/legal-data platform: its flagship RelativityOne is a cloud SaaS for processing, reviewing, and producing evidence in litigation, investigations, and compliance matters, plus Relativity Trace for communication surveillance. Ownership: private, with **Silver Lake, Iconiq Capital, and founder Andrew Sieja as equity partners, each with board representation** ([PR Newswire CEO announcement](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/relativity-welcomes-phil-saunders-as-ceo-301680942.html)). CEO is **Phil Saunders** (ex-Cornerstone OnDemand), appointed December 2022 replacing Mike Gamson ([LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2022/11/relativity-names-new-ceo.html)). Scale: **"more than 300,000 users in approximately 40 countries," thousands of organizations, including the U.S. Department of Justice and 198 of the Am Law 200** (same PR Newswire release), and **8.7 petabytes processed in RelativityOne in the trailing 12 months** as stated at Relativity Fest London 2025 ([EDRM](https://edrm.net/2025/06/relativity-fest-london-showcases-the-future-of-discoverable-data/)). Recent corporate news: acquisition of AI drafting/automation startup **Gavel (June 12, 2026)** ([Relativity newsroom](https://www.relativity.com/news-events/relativity-acquires-gavel-to-extend-its-ai-platform-for-legal-data-intelligence-into-microsoft-word/)) and launch of **Rel Labs**, an investment arm/innovation hub, at Fest 2025 ([PR Newswire](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/relativity-fest-2025-relativity-announces-generative-ai-solutions-for-legal-review-to-be-standard-in-cloud-offering-302578299.html)). **Not verified:** the widely reported 2021 Silver Lake growth investment at a ~$3.6B valuation — I could not reach a primary source this session (LawSites URL 404'd); treat the figure as unconfirmed. No official layoff/restructuring announcements found for 2025–26 (only anecdotal [Glassdoor mentions](https://www.glassdoor.com/Reviews/Relativity-layoff-Reviews-EI_IE1771815.0,10_KH11,17.htm)).

## 2. Generative AI Capabilities 2025–2026

- **aiR for Review** (GA since 2024): GPT-class LLM first-pass review for **responsiveness, issues, and key documents**. For every document it emits a **Prediction + confidence Score, a Rationale, "Considerations" (the model arguing against itself), and Citations — up to 5 verbatim excerpts highlighted in the document viewer** (orange for relevance, purple for key docs, color-mapped for issues) ([Relativity blog "Secret Weapons"](https://www.relativity.com/blog/secret-weapons-of-ai-assisted-legal-review-rationales-considerations-citations-scores/), [aiR for Review Analysis help](https://help.relativity.com/RelativityOne/Content/Relativity/Viewer/aiR_for_Review_Analysis.htm)). Claimed throughput up to **3M docs/day**; 200+ customers, 25M+ docs reviewed in year one ([Fest 2025 PR](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/relativity-fest-2025-relativity-announces-generative-ai-solutions-for-legal-review-to-be-standard-in-cloud-offering-302578299.html)). June 2026 added **no-code "custom analyses"** (arbitrary user-defined document analyses) ([Relativity newsroom](https://www.relativity.com/news-events/relativity-expands-ai-platform-for-legal-data-intelligence-with-conversational-ai-and-no-code-custom-document-analyses/)).
- **aiR for Privilege**: privilege-call automation combining GenAI with **NLP, ML, and social-network analysis; marketed as "agentic AI" that maps relationships, roles, organizations** and explains its rationale; customers report up to 80% review-time reduction ([product page](https://www.relativity.com/data-solutions/air/privilege/), [Fest 2025 PR](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/relativity-fest-2025-relativity-announces-generative-ai-solutions-for-legal-review-to-be-standard-in-cloud-offering-302578299.html)).
- **Fest 2025 (Oct 7–9)**: aiR for Review + aiR for Privilege **became standard in RelativityOne pricing/packaging**, plus built-in **statistical validation** of GenAI results, GenAI contract review and data-breach response in development, and an agentic-AI roadmap ([eDiscovery Today](https://ediscoverytoday.com/2025/10/08/relativity-announces-generative-ai-solutions-to-be-standard-in-cloud-offering/)).
- **aiR for Case Strategy**: limited availability March 2025, **GA January 2026** — fact extraction, chronologies, witness summaries, deposition outlines (details in §5) ([PR Newswire](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/relativity-launches-air-for-case-strategy-bringing-generative-ai-to-case-intelligence-302658042.html), [LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/01/relativity-launches-air-for-case-strategy-to-extract-and-organize-key-facts-using-generative-ai.html)).
- **aiR Assist** (conversational AI): announced Fest 2025, **GA June 2026 and standard in RelativityOne by end of June 2026**. Plain-language Q&A over indexed documents with **citation-backed answers, up to 25 references per question, multi-turn context**; capacity limits of **300,000 docs per index / 1.5M docs per workspace** ([Artificial Lawyer](https://www.artificiallawyer.com/2026/06/16/relativity-announces-air-assist-general-roll-out/), [aiR Assist help](https://help.relativity.com/RelativityOne/Content/Relativity/Searching/aiR_Assist/aiR_Assist.htm), [Relativity newsroom](https://www.relativity.com/news-events/relativity-expands-ai-platform-for-legal-data-intelligence-with-conversational-ai-and-no-code-custom-document-analyses/)).
- **Agentic AI**: positioned as roadmap + architecture ("engine layer where agents, skills, and tools turn data into action with governance") and marketing/education (a "Promise and Limitations of Agentic AI" toolkit); shipping agentic behavior today is mostly inside aiR for Privilege and Case Strategy's collection-gap detection ([Relativity blog](https://www.relativity.com/blog/ai-action-and-community-turning-complex-data-into-insights-at-scale/), [eDiscovery Today](https://ediscoverytoday.com/2026/02/04/the-promise-and-limitations-of-agentic-ai-artificial-intelligence-best-practices/)).
- **Provenance behavior overall**: consistent pattern — every AI output (review predictions, extracted facts, Assist answers) is anchored to verbatim excerpts/citations from source documents; Case Strategy explicitly refuses to generate a fact without a matching source passage ("If we do not find a match in the document, we're not going to generate a fact for you") ([LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/01/relativity-launches-air-for-case-strategy-to-extract-and-organize-key-facts-using-generative-ai.html)).

## 3. Entity / Relationship Analysis

**No true knowledge graph.** Relativity's entity layer is the legacy Analytics stack: **Name Normalization** parses email headers (top-level + embedded) to resolve aliases (addresses, display names) into Entity records, auto-merging entities with matching first/last names and merging with entities from Legal Hold/Processing/Case Dynamics ([help: Name normalization](https://help.relativity.com/RelativityOne/Content/Relativity/Analytics/Name_normalization.htm)). The **Communication Analysis widget** renders frequency/pattern/network visualizations between entities — but it is driven by the Entity From/Recipient fields produced by name normalization, i.e., it is **email-header-derived, not a corpus-wide graph** spanning chats, phone data, and documents ([help: Communication analysis](https://help.relativity.com/RelativityOne/Content/Relativity/Analytics/Communication_analysis.htm)). aiR for Privilege applies social-network analysis to map roles/relationships for privilege decisions ([product page](https://www.relativity.com/data-solutions/air/privilege/)), and aiR Assist marketing claims answers that "connect documents, evidence and relationships" ([newsroom](https://www.relativity.com/news-events/relativity-expands-ai-platform-for-legal-data-intelligence-with-conversational-ai-and-no-code-custom-document-analyses/)) — but **I found no evidence of a persistent cross-corpus entity graph unifying email, chat, and mobile identities**. Verdict: fundamentally **document-centric** with an email-entity overlay. This is a genuine gap vs. an AI-native graph-based investigative platform.

## 4. Evidence Types: Documents, Chat (RSMF), and Mobile/Cellebrite

- Core: documents, email, and short-message data via the proprietary **Relativity Short Message Format (RSMF)** — the de-facto industry standard for Slack/Teams/chat review, rendered in a threaded Short Message Viewer ([CDS](https://cdslegal.com/insights/the-new-era-of-chat-review-the-relativity-short-message-viewer/), [Avalon](https://teamavalon.com/avalon-blog/ediscovery-game-changer-relativity-short-message-format)).
- **Cellebrite**: a formal 2024 partnership — Cellebrite remote/endpoint mobile collections convert automatically to RSMF and load into RelativityOne ([PR Newswire](https://www.prnewswire.com/in/news-releases/relativity-and-cellebrite-partner-to-unleash-the-power-of-mobile-data-302207873.html), [Cellebrite blog](https://cellebrite.com/en/blog/the-cellebrite-relativity-partnership-sets-the-standard-for-remote-mobile-collection/)); Relativity Collect has a native Cellebrite data source ([help](https://help.relativity.com/RelativityOne/Content/Collect/Data_sources/Cellebrite.htm)).
- **But mobile data is second-class beyond chats.** Per the official processing docs ([help: Cellebrite supported file types](https://help.relativity.com/RelativityOne/Content/Relativity/Processing/SupportedFileTypes-Cellebrite.htm)): UFDR **chats** (SMS/MMS, WhatsApp, Messenger, WeChat, Telegram) → RSMF; emails → .eml; media → natives. **Calls, contacts, calendar, locations, web history, installed apps are flattened into searchable Excel spreadsheets dumped in an "Other Data" folder** — call logs are "output only as call log data in Excel format, not as individual records." Voicemail recordings unsupported; participant avatars unsupported; multi-device UFDRs unsupported. In other words: no first-class call/contact/location objects, no per-event timeline integration, no cross-artifact identity linkage for phone data. This is arguably the single biggest structural gap for an investigations-focused competitor.

## 5. Chronology / Timeline / Narrative Features

aiR for Case Strategy (GA Jan 2026) is the narrative product ([help](https://help.relativity.com/RelativityOne/Content/Relativity/aiR_for_Case_Strategy/aiR_for_Case_Strategy.htm), [LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/01/relativity-launches-air-for-case-strategy-to-extract-and-organize-key-facts-using-generative-ai.html), [eDiscovery Today](https://ediscoverytoday.com/2026/01/12/relativity-launches-air-for-case-strategy/), [Artificial Lawyer](https://www.artificiallawyer.com/2026/01/12/relativity-puts-air-for-case-strategy-on-general-release/)):
- Extracts **facts** from documents (jobs capped at **5,000 documents**), each grounded in a **citation to source text** — no source match, no fact.
- Facts are classified **helpful vs. harmful** to the case theory and ranked by significance.
- **Fact chronology + visual timeline**: zoom, date-range filtering, **"issue swim lanes,"** and gap-spotting (periods where expected activity is absent) ([help: Creating a fact chronology](https://help.relativity.com/RelativityOne/Content/Relativity/aiR_for_Case_Strategy/Creating_a_fact_chronology.htm)).
- **Witness summaries** ("360-degree" profiles of key individuals), **deposition outlines** with document-grounded questions, **transcript summaries** (deposition/hearing) flagging themes and contradictions; Fest 2025 enhancements added agentic **collection-gap detection** and fact-to-witness linking ([Fest 2025 PR](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/relativity-fest-2025-relativity-announces-generative-ai-solutions-for-legal-review-to-be-standard-in-cloud-offering-302578299.html)).
- Adoption: 50+ beta customers, ~600,000 facts extracted, ~70% time reduction claimed; available in 14 markets, Government instance H1 2026 ([LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/01/relativity-launches-air-for-case-strategy-to-extract-and-organize-key-facts-using-generative-ai.html)).
- Durable, citation-backed narrative collections: yes in the sense of a maintained fact/chronology workspace as new material is added — but it inherits the platform's evidence model, so it chronicles **documents**, not phone-level events (calls/locations live in Excel exports, per §4).

## 6. Deployment

- **RelativityOne**: multi-tenant SaaS on Microsoft Azure, per-workspace/per-GB metering (Cost Explorer), commercial regions in 14+ markets ([Relativity pricing page](https://www.relativity.com/pricing/), [LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/01/relativity-launches-air-for-case-strategy-to-extract-and-organize-key-facts-using-generative-ai.html)).
- **Relativity Server (on-prem) is being sunset**: announced Jan 2025 — **no new matters on Server after Jan 1, 2028**; existing matters continue with limited exceptions; annual Server releases continue until then (Server 2025 docs still published) ([LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2025/01/putting-a-nail-in-the-coffin-of-its-on-prem-product-relativity-sets-2028-deadline-for-all-new-cases-to-move-to-the-cloud.html), [Reveal](https://www.revealdata.com/blog/relativity-server-sunset-your-on-prem-ediscovery-path-forward-with-reveal), [Casepoint](https://www.casepoint.com/blog/relativity-retires-server-federal-agencies-enter-cloud-first-security-first-era/)). Competitors (Reveal, Casepoint) are actively poaching on-prem/agency customers over this.
- **Gov cloud**: **RelativityOne Government** on Azure Government Cloud, **FedRAMP Moderate ATO since Sept 2020** (EPA sponsor), used for federal eDiscovery/FOIA ([PR Newswire](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/relativity-achieves-fedramp-authorization-for-relativityone-government-301168043.html), [Relativity blog](https://www.relativity.com/blog/announcing-fedramp-authorization-for-relativityone-government/)). aiR capabilities reach Government later than commercial (Case Strategy: H1 2026).

## 7. Pricing / GTM

- **Models**: Pay-As-You-Go (monthly, usage-based) vs. Flex Commit 1-yr/3-yr with **discounts up to ~25%** ([Relativity blog: Pay As You Go](https://www.relativity.com/blog/introducing-pay-as-you-go-pricing-for-relativityone/), [pricing page](https://www.relativity.com/pricing/)).
- **Per-GB reference points** (Relativity does not publish rates): a UK G-Cloud 14 listing shows **£7.00/GB/month review hosting and £2.50/GB/month ECA/repository** ([G-Cloud pricing doc](https://assets.applytosupply.digitalmarketplace.service.gov.uk/g-cloud-14/documents/92977/830324046302655-pricing-document-2026-03-09-1933.pdf)); third-party estimates put processing ~$35/GB and hosting ~$25/GB/month with overall plans "$500–$25,000/month" ([Costbench](https://costbench.com/software/ai-legal-tools/relativity/) — **third-party estimates, unverified**).
- **aiR pricing**: Nov 1, 2025 brought an **all-inclusive "unlimited" per-GB framework** folding aiR into GB rates ([Relativity newsroom](https://www.relativity.com/news-events/relativity-unveils-new-pricing-model/), [IntrepidX](https://intrepidx.com/relativity-launches-unlimited-fixed-fee-pricing-for-air-with-intrepidx-leading-client-rollout/), [Cimplifi "all-in unlimited" release](https://markets.financialcontent.com/firstheritage/article/gnwcq-2025-10-9-cimplifi-first-to-market-with-all-in-unlimited-relativity-air-for-review-and-air-for-privilege-pricing-ushering-in-a-new-era-of-ai-driven-ediscovery)). Historical per-document aiR pricing of **~$0.25/doc to vendors** is reported only by a third-party review site ([AI Vortex](https://www.aivortex.io/legal/ai-tools/relativity-air/)) — **could not verify officially**. Case Strategy adds a per-workspace fee incl. 150GB deposition video ([help](https://help.relativity.com/RelativityOne/Content/Relativity/aiR_for_Case_Strategy/aiR_for_Case_Strategy.htm)).
- **Channel**: overwhelmingly sold through a **certified service-provider/partner network** (FTI, CDS, Cimplifi, Epiq, hundreds more); boutique/small firms are explicitly steered to partners, who resell capacity **without Relativity's minimum data thresholds** ([Relativity partners page](https://www.relativity.com/partners/), [Proteus on small/mid firms](https://blog.proteusdiscovery.com/relativity-for-small-and-mid-sized-law-firms), [Special Counsel](https://www.specialcounsel.com/your-relativityone-ediscovery-partner/), [Lineal](https://lineal.com/en-gb/resources/blog/why-forward-thinking-legal-teams-choose-relativityone-through-a-partner/)). **Practical answer: a small criminal-defense or PI firm cannot realistically buy RelativityOne direct** — direct licensing carries commitments sized for enterprises; small firms rent workspace-by-workspace from a hosting partner (per-GB + per-user pass-through pricing). I could not verify an exact direct minimum commitment figure.

## 8. Defense-Side vs. Plaintiff / Prosecution Usage

- **Prosecution/government**: the **U.S. Department of Justice is a named Relativity customer** ([PR Newswire CEO release](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/relativity-welcomes-phil-saunders-as-ceo-301680942.html)); RelativityOne Government's FedRAMP ATO (EPA-sponsored) serves federal agencies for eDiscovery/FOIA ([PR Newswire](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/relativity-achieves-fedramp-authorization-for-relativityone-government-301168043.html)). DOJ/federal-defender joint ESI protocols exist for criminal discovery ([justice.gov recommendations](https://www.justice.gov/archives/dag/page/file/913236/download)) but say nothing Relativity-specific.
- **Defense/social-justice side**: the **Justice for Change** program gives pro-bono social-justice matters **free RelativityOne for 24 months (500GB / 10 users)** — used by e.g. the Georgia Innocence Project ([Relativity](https://www.relativity.com/company/commitments/social-impact/justice-for-change/), [Above the Law](https://abovethelaw.com/2022/11/relativity-brings-legal-technology-to-the-struggle-for-justice/)). This is charity-tier, not a product motion.
- **Could not verify**: any systematic adoption by public defender offices or a plaintiff-vs-defense usage split; commercial usage skews to Am Law firms (198 of Am Law 200), corporate legal, and service providers. Flag: criminal-defense/PI-firm segment appears essentially unserved by direct GTM — a whitespace for an AI-native investigative platform.

## 9. Notable 2025–2026 News

- **Jan 2025**: Server sunset announced — all new matters to cloud from 2028 ([LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2025/01/putting-a-nail-in-the-coffin-of-its-on-prem-product-relativity-sets-2028-deadline-for-all-new-cases-to-move-to-the-cloud.html)).
- **Mar 2025**: aiR for Case Strategy limited availability ([Fest 2025 PR](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/relativity-fest-2025-relativity-announces-generative-ai-solutions-for-legal-review-to-be-standard-in-cloud-offering-302578299.html)).
- **Oct 2025, Relativity Fest**: aiR for Review + Privilege made **standard in RelativityOne**; statistical validation; contract-review + data-breach GenAI; agentic roadmap; **Rel Labs** investment arm; **new all-in per-GB pricing from Nov 1, 2025** ([PR Newswire](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/relativity-fest-2025-relativity-announces-generative-ai-solutions-for-legal-review-to-be-standard-in-cloud-offering-302578299.html), [Relativity newsroom pricing](https://www.relativity.com/news-events/relativity-unveils-new-pricing-model/)).
- **Jan 2026**: aiR for Case Strategy GA ([PR Newswire](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/relativity-launches-air-for-case-strategy-bringing-generative-ai-to-case-intelligence-302658042.html)).
- **Mar 2026, Legalweek**: company **brand refresh** ([Law.com](https://www.law.com/legaltechnews/2026/03/09/legalweek-2026-pre-show-news-rundown-relativity-announces-brand-refresh-epiq-expands-agentic-ai-suite-and-more-/)).
- **Jun 2026**: **Gavel acquisition** (AI drafting/contract review into Microsoft Word, syncing back to RelativityOne; Gavel team incl. founder Dorna Moini joins; terms undisclosed) ([Relativity newsroom](https://www.relativity.com/news-events/relativity-acquires-gavel-to-extend-its-ai-platform-for-legal-data-intelligence-into-microsoft-word/), [LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/06/relativity-acquires-document-automation-company-gavel-extending-its-legal-data-platform-into-word.html)); **aiR Assist GA + custom analyses, standard by end of June**; RelFest London 2026 previewed "a brand-new experience built for lawyers" ([Artificial Lawyer](https://www.artificiallawyer.com/2026/06/16/relativity-announces-air-assist-general-roll-out/), [Relativity blog](https://relativity.com/blog/reimagining-legal-what-we-announced-at-relfest-london-2026/)).
- 2024 (context): Cellebrite partnership for mobile collection→RSMF ([PR Newswire](https://www.prnewswire.com/in/news-releases/relativity-and-cellebrite-partner-to-unleash-the-power-of-mobile-data-302207873.html)).

**Explicitly unverified items**: Silver Lake 2021 deal size/valuation; official aiR per-document rate; direct-purchase minimum commitment figures; any 2025–26 layoffs; public-defender adoption; and whether aiR Assist/Assist indexes extend beyond documents to entities at the data layer (marketing implies "relationships," docs show document indexes only).

**Key competitive takeaways for an AI-native investigative platform**: (1) mobile-phone evidence (calls, contacts, locations) is flattened to Excel files, not first-class events; (2) entity resolution is email-header-scoped, with no cross-corpus knowledge graph; (3) chronology/citation UX is strong and now table stakes; (4) small defense/PI firms can't buy direct — channel-only access at enterprise-derived per-GB prices leaves that segment open.
---
## 4.2 Everlaw

# Everlaw Competitive Research Report (as of mid-2026)

## 1. Company Overview, Ownership, Funding

Everlaw is a cloud-native ediscovery, litigation, and investigation platform founded by CEO AJ Shankar, headquartered in Oakland, CA, serving law firms, corporations, and government agencies with document review, advanced analytics, generative AI, and narrative-building (Storybuilder) tools ([everlaw.com/product](https://www.everlaw.com/product/)). Its last disclosed round was a **$202M Series D (Nov 2021) led by TPG Growth, with H.I.G. Growth Partners and existing investors CapitalG, Menlo Ventures, Andreessen Horowitz, and K9 Ventures, at a valuation over $2B**; total raised ~$315M ([everlaw.com blog](https://www.everlaw.com/blog/everlaw/everlaw-secures-202-million-series-d-round-of-funding/), [TPG](https://www.tpg.com/news-and-insights/everlaw-announces-202m-series-d-fuel-ediscovery-transformation), [PR Newswire](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/everlaw-announces-202m-series-d-to-fuel-ediscovery-transformation-301413393.html)). Scale claims: **Fortune 100 corporate counsel, 91 of the Am Law 200, and all 50 state attorneys general**; "over 40,000 legal professionals" ([Everlaw press release, Mar 2026](https://www.everlaw.com/press/release/everlaw-for-good-surpasses-6-million-in-donated-technology/), [everlaw.com/law-firms](https://www.everlaw.com/law-firms/)). **IPO/acquisition: no filing found.** Pre-IPO secondary-market pages exist ([Forge](https://forgeglobal.com/everlaw_ipo/), [Nasdaq Private Market](https://www.nasdaqprivatemarket.com/company/everlaw/)), and a CapitalG board member has said they're "betting on [Everlaw] being on a path to go public," but I found **no confirmed 2025–2026 funding, valuation update, IPO filing, or acquisition news** — the $2B valuation is from Nov 2021 and may be stale. *Flag: no post-2021 valuation could be verified.*

## 2. Generative AI Capabilities (2025–2026)

Everlaw's GenAI suite ("EverlawAI Assistant") now has four main pillars, with 250+ customers using GenAI features as of Oct 2025 ([Businesswire, Oct 22 2025](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20251022355685/en/Everlaw-Launches-Deep-Dive-Fundamentally-Reshaping-Legal-Document-Review---Helping-Legal-Teams-to-Know-It-Now)):

- **Review Assistant** — per-document and batch: citation-backed summaries, topic analysis, custom data extractions, sentiment analysis, document Q&A, and **Coding Suggestions** (claimed precision 0.77 / recall 0.82, "surpassing first-level human review by 36%") ([everlaw.com Review Assistant](https://www.everlaw.com/product/everlaw-ai/review-assistant/), [Getting started KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/25399857057691-Getting-started-with-Everlaw-AI-Assistant)).
- **Deep Dive (GA Dec 2025)** — the headline Summit '25 launch: **open-ended natural-language Q&A across the entire corpus** (tested on tens of millions of docs / terabytes), answers ranked by confidence, **citation-backed with lists of facts and referenceable source documents**, and explicit "insufficient evidence" responses instead of hallucinating. Priced as a one-time per-GB ingestion fee with unlimited questions for the life of the case ([Businesswire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20251022355685/en/Everlaw-Launches-Deep-Dive-Fundamentally-Reshaping-Legal-Document-Review---Helping-Legal-Teams-to-Know-It-Now), [LawSites, Nov 2025](https://www.lawnext.com/2025/11/everlaw-announces-general-availability-of-ai-deep-dive-as-well-as-major-pricing-changes-at-annual-summit.html)).
- **Writing Assistant (Storybuilder)** — generates first drafts/outlines, event timelines, deposition questions, and fact analyses from user-selected evidence ([Storybuilder and Writing Assistant KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/25402606829083-Storybuilder-and-Writing-Assistant)).
- **Deposition Analyzer** — transcript analysis with pre-built summary/analysis tasks plus custom questions; "Depositions Q&A" and a Privilege Descriptions tool were announced as upcoming ([Everlaw trial prep](https://www.everlaw.com/trial-preparation/), [LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2025/11/everlaw-announces-general-availability-of-ai-deep-dive-as-well-as-major-pricing-changes-at-annual-summit.html)).

**Agentic:** Summit '25 introduced **Everlaw Workflows / Workflow Builder** (early-stage: repeatable workflows with automated triggering, conditional branching, human approval gates) ([LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2025/11/everlaw-announces-general-availability-of-ai-deep-dive-as-well-as-major-pricing-changes-at-annual-summit.html)), and CTO Max Christoff has publicly framed agentic AI as law's "breakout season" ([Everlaw Summit keynote blog](https://www.everlaw.com/blog/everlaw-summit/keynote-reimagining-workflows-gen-ai/)). In **May 2026 Everlaw shipped an Anthropic MCP integration**: Claude can search Everlaw case data, retrieve documents by Bates number, analyze metadata, and generate reports/visualizations under the user's existing Everlaw permissions, explicitly marketed as enabling "agentic workflows" ([Everlaw blog](https://www.everlaw.com/blog/ai-and-advanced-analytics/anthropic-mcp-integration/), [LawSites, May 2026](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/05/anthropic-goes-all-in-on-legal-releasing-more-than-20-connectors-and-12-practice-area-plugins-for-claude.html)). **Provenance behavior: consistently citation-first** — AI outputs cite source documents for verification, and Everlaw uses enterprise LLMs under zero-data-retention terms ([AI Governance Framework](https://www.everlaw.com/product/everlaw-ai-assistant/framework/)).

## 3. Entity / Relationship Analysis

More than search-and-review, but **no true knowledge graph**:

- **Communication Visualizer** — an interactive, filterable **network graph of email communications** explicitly "based on the concept of social network analysis": nodes are people sized by correspondence volume, edges sized by frequency, filterable over time; can run on any slice or cluster of the corpus ([KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/17106075039771-Communication-Visualizer), [Everlaw blog](https://www.everlaw.com/blog/ediscovery-software/introducing-communication-visualizer/)).
- **Entity Management / entity resolution** — Everlaw auto-groups contacts (email addresses + names) into "entities" per individual, matching shared name words while filtering common non-name words; admins can **merge entities, correct misattributions, and filter out distribution lists** ([Entity Management KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/29564010077083-Entity-Management)). Metadata fields are auto-normalized (Custodian/Custdn/CUST grouped) with user-defined aliases ([KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/204913069-Project-Settings-Metadata-Metadata-Fields-Aliases-Editable-Metadata-and-Other-Settings)).
- **Clustering** — unsupervised concept clustering up to **25M documents** on a single dashboard ([everlaw.com Clustering](https://www.everlaw.com/product/everlaw-ai/clustering/)), plus standard email threading and predictive coding ([product page](https://www.everlaw.com/product/)).

**Gap for comparison purposes:** the relationship model is email-metadata-centric (senders/recipients). I found **no persistent cross-corpus knowledge graph, no relationship extraction from message content, and entity resolution is contact/alias-level, not full identity resolution across data types** — *this is an inference from documented features; Everlaw does not publish anything describing a knowledge graph.*

## 4. Evidence Types & Mobile Phone Extraction

- **Broad type support**: documents, email, spreadsheets, audio/video (with transcription), Slack (via a Cloud Connector app that filters by custodian/date and pulls DMs, public and private channels — [Slack Marketplace](https://slack.com/marketplace/A01K2PQ55FW-everlaw-cloud-connector)), and **RSMF** short-message data (Slack/Teams/SMS/WhatsApp normalized) — Everlaw processes the RSMF JSON into chat files with linked attachments ([Supported Native Data Types KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/360026908431-Supported-Native-Data-Types)).
- **Cellebrite UFED: natively supported.** Everlaw ingests **UFDR files or zipped Cellebrite export folders (with XML report)** directly ([Upload Cellebrite Exports KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/360051062431-Upload-Cellebrite-Exports) — *note: the KB page itself blocked fetching (403), details corroborated via search excerpts and Everlaw's guide*).
- **How phone data is treated** ([Handling Mobile Data guide](https://www.everlaw.com/guides/novel-data-types/handling-mobile-data-in-ediscovery/)): chats/SMS/MMS render **as chat bubbles with app-specific formatting** (iMessage, WhatsApp) with inline images and non-image attachments as child documents — so chats are first-class-ish in the viewer. However, **conversations are segmented into documents (auto-split per 1,000 messages, with a tool for smaller splits)**, and **call logs and contacts are surfaced as spreadsheet-format documents with metadata** — i.e., phone artifacts are ultimately mapped onto the document-review paradigm, not modeled as structured call/contact entities with graph semantics. This is a meaningful differentiation point versus a phone-extraction-native platform.

## 5. Storybuilder: Chronology / Narrative Features

Storybuilder is Everlaw's narrative toolkit, included with the platform ([Intro to Storybuilder KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/206439403-Introduction-to-Storybuilder)):

- **Fact Timelines** — structured chronological workspace of "Facts" (discrete events), each linkable to **supporting evidence documents, people, labels, Depositions, Drafts, and related Facts** ([Fact Timelines KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/42469701435803-Storybuilder-Fact-Timelines)).
- **Depositions** — prepare, conduct, summarize, and annotate transcripts; integrated task management, outlines, deposition questions, version control ([Depositions KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/43716908407963-Introduction-to-Depositions-on-Everlaw), [Deposition Preparation KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/360038816332-Deposition-Preparation)).
- **Drafts** — collaborative word processor that **links directly to key evidence and Facts** ([KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/206439403-Introduction-to-Storybuilder)).
- **AI in Storybuilder** — Writing Assistant generates timelines, deposition-question drafts, and fact analyses from selected evidence; now bundled free ([Storybuilder + Writing Assistant KB](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/25402606829083-Storybuilder-and-Writing-Assistant)).

**Assessment vs. "durable investigator-built narrative collections":** Storybuilder genuinely delivers durable, evidence-cited chronologies and narratives — this is the closest analogue in mainstream ediscovery to investigator narrative collections. Its facts cite documents, but it remains **case/project-scoped, document-citation-based, and litigation-drafting-oriented**; it does not appear to model entities/events as reusable graph objects across matters. *(Inference from documentation; not an Everlaw statement.)*

## 6. Deployment

- **Multi-tenant SaaS on AWS**; AWS is the sole infrastructure partner ([everlaw.com/partners/aws](https://www.everlaw.com/partners/aws/)).
- **FedRAMP Moderate authorized** (Agency ATO path, NIST 800-53 Rev 5) and available in a **FedRAMP/StateRAMP-authorized AWS GovCloud (US)** environment operated by U.S. citizens on U.S. soil (supports NOFORN) — migrated to GovCloud in 2021 ([Government Compliance FAQ](https://www.everlaw.com/government-compliance/faq/), [Above the Law](https://abovethelaw.com/2021/09/everlaw-migrates-federal-saas-platform-to-aws-govcloud/), [ComplexDiscovery](https://complexdiscovery.com/everlaw-moves-to-aws-govcloud-us/)).
- ISO 27001, SOC 2 Type 2, HIPAA and GDPR audit support ([Government Compliance FAQ](https://www.everlaw.com/government-compliance/faq/)).
- **On-prem / self-hosted: no evidence of any option** — everything published points to cloud-only SaaS (commercial cloud + GovCloud instance). *Flag: I could not find an explicit "no on-prem" statement, but no on-prem offering is documented anywhere.*

## 7. Pricing & GTM

- **Model:** data-hosted per-GB pricing with **unlimited users, processing, and productions** included; pay-as-you-go or annual committed-volume subscriptions at lower rates ([Everlaw pricing blog](https://www.everlaw.com/blog/ediscovery-software/how-everlaws-pricing-is-different/), [Billing FAQ](https://support.everlaw.com/hc/en-us/articles/205660765-Billing-and-Pricing-FAQ)). No public price list; sales-negotiated.
- **Third-party cost signals (unverified by Everlaw):** ~$18–35/GB/month plus base subscriptions cited at $2,000–5,000+/month depending on size ([Spendbase](https://www.spendbase.co/vendors/everlaw/), [AI Vortex](https://www.aivortex.io/legal/compare/everlaw-pricing-2026/)). *Flag: these are aggregator estimates, not official.*
- **2025 pricing shift:** Writing Assistant, Deposition Analyzer, and single-doc Review Assistant folded into core per-GB pricing at no extra charge; batch Coding Suggestions cut 40%+; no per-GB increases ([LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2025/11/everlaw-announces-general-availability-of-ai-deep-dive-as-well-as-major-pricing-changes-at-annual-summit.html)).
- **Everlaw for Good:** free access up to a monthly usage cap (heavily discounted beyond), unlimited licenses/cases, incl. processing, transcription, translation, Storybuilder, and AI Assistant credits, plus free support/training; eligible: 501(c)(3) nonprofits, legal aid, pro bono practices, investigative journalists, educators/law clinics, and — notably — **CJA panel attorneys in criminal defense**; program has surpassed **$6M–$7M in donated services, 235–300+ organizations, 675–900+ matters**, growing 74% YoY ([everlaw.com/everlaw-for-good](https://www.everlaw.com/everlaw-for-good/), [press release Mar 2026](https://www.everlaw.com/press/release/everlaw-for-good-surpasses-6-million-in-donated-technology/), [ABA profile](https://www.americanbar.org/groups/center-pro-bono/publications/pro-bono-exchange/2024/everlawforgood/)).
- **Can a small criminal-defense/PI firm buy it?** Realistically marginal at commercial rates (enterprise-oriented pricing per aggregators), **but** CJA panel attorneys can get it free/discounted via Everlaw for Good, and the unlimited-seats per-GB model makes small-data matters feasible. *Assessment partly inferential — Everlaw publishes no SMB pricing.*

## 8. Defense-Side vs. Plaintiff/Prosecution Usage

- **All 50 state attorneys general** use Everlaw ([Everlaw press boilerplate](https://www.everlaw.com/press/release/everlaw-for-good-surpasses-6-million-in-donated-technology/)) — i.e., heavy state-prosecution/enforcement penetration; federal agencies served via GovCloud/FedRAMP ([Government Compliance FAQ](https://www.everlaw.com/government-compliance/faq/)).
- **Public defenders:** documented use by "NY's largest public defender organization" (housing-rights matter) and positioning of Everlaw for due-process/discovery review by defenders; the 2024 Everlaw for Good expansion explicitly added **CJA panel attorneys** (federal indigent criminal defense) ([Businesswire, May 2024](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20240509238116/en/Everlaw-Expands-its-Everlaw-for-Good-Program-to-Level-the-Playing-Field-for-Justice), [everlaw-for-good page](https://www.everlaw.com/everlaw-for-good/)).
- **Plaintiff firms:** Everlaw maintains a plaintiffs'-firms content track and is resold by plaintiff-only providers like ILS ([Everlaw plaintiffs' blog](https://www.everlaw.com/blog/plaintiffs-firms/), [ILS](https://www.ilsteam.com/everlaw-ai-assistant/)).
- *Flag: I could not verify named criminal-defense firm customers or specific public defender offices beyond the above; Everlaw does not publish a defense-side customer list.*

## 9. Notable 2025–2026 News

- **Oct 2025 — Max Christoff appointed first CTO** (ex-Google Chrome engineering lead); R&D headcount to grow ~30% by Jan 2026; GenAI investment up 50% since 2024 ([Everlaw press](https://www.everlaw.com/press/release/everlaw-appoints-max-christoff-as-chief-technology-officer/), [Legaltech News](https://www.law.com/legaltechnews/2025/10/20/everlaws-new-cto-on-making-the-leap-to-legal-tech-and-investing-in-research-/)).
- **Oct–Dec 2025 — Everlaw Summit '25**: Deep Dive GA, Workflow Builder preview, AI bundled into core pricing, upcoming Depositions Q&A + Privilege Descriptions ([Businesswire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20251022355685/en/Everlaw-Launches-Deep-Dive-Fundamentally-Reshaping-Legal-Document-Review---Helping-Legal-Teams-to-Know-It-Now), [LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2025/11/everlaw-announces-general-availability-of-ai-deep-dive-as-well-as-major-pricing-changes-at-annual-summit.html), [Everlaw Summit keynote](https://www.everlaw.com/blog/everlaw-summit/keynote-reimagining-workflows-gen-ai/)).
- **Mar 2026 — Everlaw for Good passes $6M donated**, journalism usage ~4x ([press release](https://www.everlaw.com/press/release/everlaw-for-good-surpasses-6-million-in-donated-technology/)).
- **May 2026 — Anthropic MCP integration** ships as part of Anthropic's "Claude for Legal" launch (alongside Relativity and Consilio connectors) ([Everlaw blog](https://www.everlaw.com/blog/ai-and-advanced-analytics/anthropic-mcp-integration/), [Artificial Lawyer](https://www.artificiallawyer.com/2026/05/12/claude-for-legal-launches-may-reshape-the-legal-tech-world/), [LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/05/anthropic-goes-all-in-on-legal-releasing-more-than-20-connectors-and-12-practice-area-plugins-for-claude.html)).
- **No layoffs found in 2025–2026** (last known: 10% cut in May 2023 — [Legaltech News](https://www.law.com/legaltechnews/2023/05/12/everlaw-lays-off-10-of-global-workforce-to-prepare-for-economic-uncertainty/)). *Flag: absence of layoff news is not proof of none.*

## Key Competitive Takeaways for an AI-Native Investigative Platform

1. **Corpus-wide cited Q&A is now table stakes**: Deep Dive delivers citation-backed, confidence-ranked open Q&A over terabyte corpora, priced per-GB once per case.
2. **Everlaw's weakest flank is exactly the investigative-graph space**: mobile extractions are flattened into chat-bubble documents + spreadsheet call logs/contacts; entity resolution is email-contact-level; no cross-artifact knowledge graph or identity model — the strongest differentiation surface for a Cellebrite-native, graph-first platform.
3. **Storybuilder is the benchmark for evidence-cited chronologies** and now generates them with AI for free — a narrative feature must exceed it (durable entities, cross-case reuse, phone-artifact citations) rather than match it.
4. **Everlaw for Good + CJA coverage** means the defense/nonprofit segment is not undefended; pricing (per-GB, unlimited seats) is the wedge Everlaw uses down-market.

Items I could not verify: any post-2021 valuation/funding event, IPO filing or acquisition talks; single-tenant/on-prem options (none documented); official per-GB price points; named criminal-defense or public-defender customer list.

## 4.3 DISCO Cecilia

# CS DISCO (DISCO eDiscovery + Cecilia AI) — Competitive Research Report (as of July 2026)

## 1. What it is / company status

CS Disco, Inc. ("DISCO") is an Austin-based, cloud-native litigation technology company offering eDiscovery, legal document review, legal hold/request, deposition management, timelines, and AI (Cecilia) products, sold primarily to law firms and corporate legal teams. It remains an **independent public company on NYSE under ticker LAW** — no acquisition or going-private transaction was found in 2025–2026 despite searching specifically for it; the company filed a FY2025 10-K and gave FY2026 guidance as a standalone company ([stocktitan 10-K note](https://www.stocktitan.net/sec-filings/LAW/10-k-cs-disco-inc-files-annual-report-825c91852719.html), [Simply Wall St](https://simplywall.st/stocks/us/software/nyse-law/cs-disco)). Scale: FY2025 total revenue **$156.8M** (+8% YoY), software revenue $134.0M (+12%), net loss $10.7M, cash $114.6M with zero debt ([Q4/FY2025 results PDF](https://s28.q4cdn.com/612709996/files/doc_financials/2025/q4/DISCO-Announces-Fourth-Quarter-and-Fiscal-Year-2025-Financial-Results.pdf), [Yahoo Finance earnings call summary](https://finance.yahoo.com/news/cs-disco-q4-earnings-call-164131997.html)). Market cap is small — **~$233M as of March 2026**, down ~50% over the prior 12 months ([companiesmarketcap.com](https://companiesmarketcap.com/cs-disco/marketcap/), [Macrotrends](https://www.macrotrends.net/stocks/charts/LAW/cs-disco/market-cap)) — i.e., trading near ~1.4x revenue. Leadership: CEO **Eric Friedrichsen** (ex-Emburse), appointed April 2024 after co-founder Kiwi Camara resigned in Sept 2023 amid misconduct allegations and an interim period under Scott Hill ([legal.io](https://www.legal.io/articles/5491185/CS-Disco-Appoints-New-CEO-to-Steer-Company-Post-Controversy)); Chief Product Officer is Richard Crum ([LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/02/disco-launches-scaled-agentic-ai-tool-for-large-discovery-and-fact-investigation-matters.html)).

## 2. Generative-AI capabilities 2025–2026 (Cecilia family)

Per DISCO's official Cecilia page ([csdisco.com/offerings/cecilia](https://csdisco.com/offerings/cecilia)) and AI features page ([csdisco.com/offerings/ediscovery/features-ai](https://csdisco.com/offerings/ediscovery/features-ai)):

- **Cecilia Q&A (evidence-level)** — "Ask a question about your case, and get an answer supported by cited documents." DISCO explicitly markets that answers are "crafted only from your evidence... with citations always included" — citation/provenance is a core, advertised behavior.
- **Cecilia Q&A for individual documents** — per-document interrogation, excerpt identification, QC of tagging decisions.
- **Cecilia Auto Review** — GenAI document tagging from plain-English tag descriptions, returning "tagged documents with narrative justifications for each decision." Priced **per document** (separate from per-GB) ([csdisco.com/pricing](https://csdisco.com/pricing)).
- **Cecilia Doc Summaries** — one-click summaries in the viewer, including foreign-language documents.
- **Cecilia Auto Timelines** — upload a complaint/case document, get an AI-generated legal timeline "with citations included" ([auto-timelines page](https://csdisco.com/offerings/cecilia/auto-timelines)); AI-extracted facts link to exact source sentences with in-viewer highlighting ([Case Builder support doc](https://cbsupport.csdisco.com/hc/en-us/articles/21196890780429-Cecilia-Auto-Timelines)).
- **Cecilia Deposition Summaries** — automated summaries of transcripts "by chronology and topic."
- **Agentic AI (Feb 2026)** — DISCO announced "the industry's first **scaled agentic AI** tool for fact investigation and eDiscovery": an autonomous multi-step reasoning engine layered onto Cecilia Q&A, aimed at millions-of-documents/terabyte-scale corpora, generally available "later in 2026" **at no additional cost** ([DISCO press release](https://csdisco.com/pressrelease/disco-first-to-bring-scaled-agentic-ai-to-legal-tech), [LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/02/disco-launches-scaled-agentic-ai-tool-for-large-discovery-and-fact-investigation-matters.html)).
- Data handling: DISCO states Cecilia's LLMs "never train on or learn from customer data" and don't retain data post-query ([features-ai](https://csdisco.com/offerings/ediscovery/features-ai)).
- Traction: Cecilia AI + Auto Review revenue grew **>600% YoY in Q4 2025** (off a small base) and adoption grew >300% since Sept 2024 ([Yahoo](https://finance.yahoo.com/news/cs-disco-q4-earnings-call-164131997.html), [LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/02/disco-launches-scaled-agentic-ai-tool-for-large-discovery-and-fact-investigation-matters.html)).

## 3. Entity/relationship analysis

**Document-search/review centric — no knowledge graph or entity-resolution product found.** DISCO's own AI/analytics feature page lists topic clustering, search visualizations, email-conversation ("most inclusive email") threading, predictive tagging, and cross-matter AI models, but makes **no mention** of communication network visualization, social network analysis, entity extraction/resolution, people analytics, or a knowledge graph ([features-ai](https://csdisco.com/offerings/ediscovery/features-ai)); the main eDiscovery product page likewise has no relationship/communication analytics ([csdisco.com/offerings/ediscovery](https://csdisco.com/offerings/ediscovery)). Targeted searches for DISCO + network analysis/entity resolution returned only third-party generic content (e.g., [Linkurious on graph tech in eDiscovery](https://linkurious.com/blog/graph-technology-ediscovery-solutions/)), not DISCO features. The Feb 2026 agentic Cecilia is positioned as finding "nuanced connections" via reasoning over search — not as a persistent graph. **Flag:** absence of evidence isn't proof of absence — there may be minor comms-visualization UI in-product not surfaced in marketing/support pages I could access — but nothing in any official page or press coverage reviewed indicates a graph/entity layer. This is a clear differentiation opportunity vs. an investigative platform built on entity/relationship modeling.

## 4. Evidence types; mobile phone extraction

- **Core:** documents, email (PST expansion, dedupe, threading, OCR — full processing included; hundreds of file types) ([2017 DISCO pricing datasheet PDF](https://images.g2crowd.com/uploads/pricing/file/10466/expirable-direct-uploads_2F90ccd58f-4299-4e9e-94d4-0e778b89ac38_2FdataSheet_pricing.pdf), [supported file types article — access blocked, existence verified](https://support.csdisco.com/hc/en-us/articles/204759684-DISCO-supported-file-types)).
- **Slack:** first-party **Collect for Slack** (Oct 2022) — Legal Hold API + Discovery API integration for hold/collect/preview/promote to review; press release promised future connectors but did not name Teams ([press release](https://csdisco.com/pressrelease/disco-launches-collect-for-slack)).
- **Mobile/short message:** DISCO's mobile eDiscovery guide says the platform "processes and renders mobile data" and "handles SMS, MMS, and messaging app data with appropriate threading, participant identification, and media rendering" — i.e., chat is rendered conversationally, not just flattened ([DISCO mobile data guide](https://csdisco.com/blog/mastering-mobile-data-for-ediscovery)).
- **Cellebrite/UFED — COULD NOT VERIFY.** No public DISCO page reviewed names Cellebrite, UFED, or UFDR. Mobile forensic ingestion appears to run through **DISCO Professional Services** ("Forensic Services & Collections — data acquisition services" on the [eDiscovery page](https://csdisco.com/offerings/ediscovery); an "[Advanced file types supported by DISCO Professional Services](https://support.csdisco.com/hc/en-us/articles/360041533651-Advanced-file-types-supported-by-DISCO-Professional-Services)" support article exists but returned HTTP 403 to fetch). **Flag:** whether DISCO ingests UFDR natively vs. converting via services, and how first-class phone artifacts (calls, contacts, locations, app data) survive, is unverified — but nothing suggests phone extractions are a first-class native object model the way they would be in a forensic tool; the marketing treats mobile as another message-review source. (For contrast, Cellebrite's own eDiscovery bridge, Legalview, targets RelativityOne, not DISCO — [CloudNine/Cellebrite coverage](https://cloudnine.com/ediscoverydaily/ediscovery-mobile-device-data-what-to-expect-from-your-ediscovery-solution/), [cellebrite.com Physical Analyzer](https://cellebrite.com/en/products/physical-analyzer/).)

## 5. Chronology/timeline/narrative features

DISCO Timelines (part of what was **Case Builder**, now folded into the platform bundle) is genuinely close to durable, investigator-built narrative collections: a cloud-based collaborative chronology where teams create facts/allegations with inline editing; dates can be exact, approximate, ranges, or undated, all searchable/sortable; facts **link directly to supporting evidence** with drag-and-drop from the doc viewer; timelines sync with DISCO Ediscovery; and it persists "from the beginning of a case until trial" ([Timelines product page](https://csdisco.com/offerings/timelines), [Timelines Overview support doc](https://cbsupport.csdisco.com/hc/en-us/articles/13962057895693-Timelines-Overview)). **Cecilia Auto Timelines** bootstraps this with GenAI: facts extracted from an uploaded complaint/document, each fact traceable to "the exact sentences that resulted in a fact" with source highlighted in a side-by-side viewer ([Cecilia Auto Timelines support doc](https://cbsupport.csdisco.com/hc/en-us/articles/21196890780429-Cecilia-Auto-Timelines)). Deposition management is bundled alongside ([Feb 2026 platform release](https://csdisco.com/pressrelease/disco-announces-all-inclusive-platform-for-ediscovery)). Gaps vs. an investigative narrative product: it's chronology-shaped (date-keyed facts), document-cited but not entity-linked, and there's no sign of typed evidence collections beyond timelines/facts.

## 6. Deployment

**Multi-tenant SaaS on AWS only.** DISCO is "powered by AWS," cloud-native from inception, SOC 2 Type 2 / ISO 27001 certified, GDPR compliant, TLS 1.2+ in transit and encryption at rest ([security features page](https://csdisco.com/offerings/ediscovery/features-security), [csdisco.com/security](https://csdisco.com/security)). No on-prem or single-tenant/private-cloud option was found on any official page — **flag: not conclusively disproven, but no evidence of any deployment mode other than its AWS SaaS**; the agentic AI is likewise described as "cloud-native" ([press release](https://csdisco.com/pressrelease/disco-first-to-bring-scaled-agentic-ai-to-legal-tech)). Regional expansion: Cecilia launched for EU/UK customers Oct 2024 ([Business Wire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20241015661201/en/DISCO-Officially-Launches-its-Cecilia-AI-Platform-in-Europe)).

## 7. Pricing / GTM

- **Model (Feb 2026):** one transparent **per-GB price on processed data, no ingest fees**, all-inclusive of Cecilia Q&A, Auto Timelines, Deposition Summaries, ECA + Active Review hosting, unlimited users — Auto Review is the exception, billed **per document** ([csdisco.com/pricing](https://csdisco.com/pricing), [platform press release](https://csdisco.com/pressrelease/disco-announces-all-inclusive-platform-for-ediscovery), [ediscovery pricing page](https://csdisco.com/offerings/ediscovery/pricing)). Billing is on top-level parent-file size (no data-expansion or production-size charges), a long-standing DISCO stance ([2017 pricing datasheet](https://images.g2crowd.com/uploads/pricing/file/10466/expirable-direct-uploads_2F90ccd58f-4299-4e9e-94d4-0e778b89ac38_2FdataSheet_pricing.pdf), [LawNext directory](https://directory.lawnext.com/products/disco/pricing/)). Project management services ran $250/hr in the older datasheet. **Flag: current per-GB dollar amounts are not public** — every page ends in "Get a quote" (TrustRadius pricing page was inaccessible, 403).
- **Earnings-call GTM shift:** in 2026 DISCO is bundling Cecilia on every matter and moving pricing toward ongoing data-size growth rather than initial load, explicitly to "reduce sales friction and improve win rates" ([Yahoo earnings call](https://finance.yahoo.com/news/cs-disco-q4-earnings-call-164131997.html)).
- **Can a small firm buy direct?** Historically yes in principle — DISCO's roots are small/mid-litigation-boutique friendly ("we can use it in any case, big or small" — Eckland & Blando quote in DISCO's own datasheet; 400+ firms incl. 75 of AmLaw 200 as of 2017), and its law-firm page features solo/boutique testimonials ([vertical/law-firms](https://csdisco.com/vertical/law-firms)). But revenue is concentrated upmarket: 330 customers at $100K+ ARR = 76% of revenue; 20 customers at $1M+ ([Yahoo](https://finance.yahoo.com/news/cs-disco-q4-earnings-call-164131997.html)). **Flags:** no published minimums, no flat-fee small-matter SKU found, and **no criminal-defense or PI-firm positioning anywhere** on official pages — a small criminal-defense/PI firm would face a quote-driven sales process with unknown minimums.

## 8. Defense-side vs plaintiff/prosecution usage

DISCO serves **both sides of civil litigation**: named segments are AmLaw 200 / defense-oriented big law, corporate in-house teams, IP litigators, and a dedicated **plaintiff mass-tort/class-action** solution ([csdisco.com/offerings/ediscovery](https://csdisco.com/offerings/ediscovery), [vertical/law-firms](https://csdisco.com/vertical/law-firms)). Case studies span boutiques (Negretti & Associates, Lynn Pinker) to Manatt. **Flags:** no quantified plaintiff-vs-defense split is disclosed anywhere I could find; **no criminal defense or prosecution presence discoverable** — the "investigations" language on the Feb 2026 platform release refers to corporate/regulatory investigations, not law-enforcement or criminal-defense work. This is essentially white space relative to a criminal/investigative evidence platform.

## 9. Notable 2025–2026 news

- **Q3 2025:** revenue $40.9M, +13% YoY; Cecilia adoption +300% since Sept 2024; **Mourant partnership** (offshore professional services firm) ([LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/02/disco-launches-scaled-agentic-ai-tool-for-large-discovery-and-fact-investigation-matters.html)).
- **Q4/FY2025 (reported Feb 2026):** Q4 revenue $41.2M (+11%); Cecilia+Auto Review revenue **+600% YoY**; large-matter revenue +30%; FY2026 guidance $167–177M with **adjusted-EBITDA breakeven targeted by Q4 2026**; CEO claims path to "20%+ grower" ([results PDF](https://s28.q4cdn.com/612709996/files/doc_financials/2025/q4/DISCO-Announces-Fourth-Quarter-and-Fiscal-Year-2025-Financial-Results.pdf), [Yahoo](https://finance.yahoo.com/news/cs-disco-q4-earnings-call-164131997.html)).
- **Feb 9, 2026:** "DISCO First to Bring Scaled Agentic AI to Legal Tech" ([press release](https://csdisco.com/pressrelease/disco-first-to-bring-scaled-agentic-ai-to-legal-tech), [Nasdaq](https://www.nasdaq.com/press-release/disco-first-bring-scaled-agentic-ai-legal-tech-2026-02-09)).
- **Feb 25, 2026 (Legalweek):** all-inclusive platform launch — eDiscovery + Cecilia + deposition management + timelines at one per-GB price, agentic AI included free ([Business Wire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260225417927/en/DISCO-Announces-All-Inclusive-Platform-for-eDiscovery)).
- **Oct 2024:** Cecilia EU/UK launch ([Business Wire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20241015661201/en/DISCO-Officially-Launches-its-Cecilia-AI-Platform-in-Europe)).
- **Acquisition rumors:** none found for 2025–2026; the company continues filing as an independent public co. **Flag:** older (2023-era) strategic-review chatter was not re-verifiable this session. Insider buying by the CEO was filed in 2025–2026 ([stocktitan Form 4](https://www.stocktitan.net/sec-filings/LAW/form-4-cs-disco-inc-insider-trading-activity-e56a21549fcd.html)). Glassdoor reviews reference layoffs through mid-2025 but **no formal layoff announcement was verifiable** ([Glassdoor](https://www.glassdoor.com/Reviews/Disco-layoff-Reviews-EI_IE6608908.0,5_KH6,12.htm)).

### Explicitly unverified items
1. Cellebrite/UFED/UFDR native ingestion — support docs exist but returned 403; no public confirmation either way.
2. Current per-GB dollar pricing, contract minimums, and any small-matter/flat-fee SKU.
3. Whether any single-tenant/private deployment exists (no evidence found; likely SaaS-only).
4. Quantified plaintiff-vs-defense revenue mix; any criminal-defense usage.
5. Scale/formality of 2025 layoffs (anecdotal Glassdoor only).

**Competitive takeaways for an AI-native investigative platform:** DISCO is document-review-centric with strong, citation-first GenAI and a genuinely good timeline/fact product, now bundled at one per-GB price with "free" agentic AI — but it has no entity/relationship graph, no first-class phone-extraction model, no criminal/investigative GTM, SaaS-only deployment, and a sub-$250M market cap under pressure to hit breakeven.

# Competitive Research Report: Reveal (Reveal-Brainspace / Reveal AI / Logikcull / IPRO) — as of mid-2026

## 1. What it is, ownership, scale, acquisition history

Reveal is a Chicago-headquartered, AI-centric eDiscovery and investigations platform company, majority-owned by K1 Investment Management. K1 backed the January 2021 Reveal–Brainspace merger with a $200M investment ([LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2023/08/in-a-1b-e-discovery-acquisition-double-play-reveal-acquires-both-logikcull-and-ipro.html)), and in August 2023 funded Reveal's simultaneous acquisition of Logikcull and IPRO in transactions valued at over $1 billion ([Businesswire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20230829922415/en/Reveal-Acquires-Logikcull-and-IPRO), [Legal IT Insider](https://legaltechnology.com/breaking-news-reveal-acquires-logikcull-and-ipro-in-transactions-valued-at-over-1bn/), [K1](https://k1.com/reveal-acquires-logikcull-and-ipro/)). Full acquisition chronology per Reveal's own about page: Mindseye (2019), NexLP/"Reveal AI" (2020), Brainspace (2021), Technically Creative (2022), LIGL (2023), Logikcull + IPRO (2023), Onna (May 2024) ([revealdata.com/about-us](https://www.revealdata.com/about-us), [Onna release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20240507774586/en/Reveal-Acquires-Onna-Further-Bolstering-Its-AI-Powered-eDiscovery-Platform-With-Superior-Data-Collection)), plus acquisition of the Blackout redaction source code from Milyli in late 2024 ([Blackout release](https://markets.financialcontent.com/worldnow.ktul/article/bizwire-2025-6-24-blackout-redaction-now-live-on-reveal-enterprise-platform)). Scale claims: 4,000+ customers in 50+ countries, 500+ employees, HQs in Chicago, Amsterdam and Hyderabad ([about-us](https://www.revealdata.com/about-us)). **Not verified:** any new funding round, valuation event, or sale/IPO process in 2025–2026 — ComplexDiscovery's M&A tracker through July 2026 lists nothing for Reveal/K1 after the 2023 deals ([ComplexDiscovery M&A tracker](https://complexdiscovery.com/ediscovery-ma-tracker-update-nine-deals-and-two-themes/)).

## 2. Generative-AI capabilities 2025–2026

- **Ask** (launched Jan 2024): natural-language generative Q&A over the review dataset; returns narrative answers with document titles, excerpts, and direct links ([Reveal blog](https://www.revealdata.com/blog/the-power-of-ai-in-ediscovery-with-reveal), [launch PR](https://markets.financialcontent.com/worldnow.ktul/article/bizwire-2024-1-10-reveal-debuts-ask-a-generative-ai-feature-designed-for-legal), [product page](https://www.revealdata.com/product/generative-ai)).
- **Citation/provenance behavior (documented, important for competitive positioning):** Ask surfaces the **top 100 sources ranked by semantic similarity**, with text-passage references and click-through to full documents; answers are confined to the matter's data and Ask says when it can't find an answer. Reveal's own docs state Ask is "**designed for precision, not recall**" — it cannot query metadata, cannot find "all" instances, struggles with information scattered across many documents, and users are told to always validate against sources ([docs: How to Ask Properly](https://docs.revealdata.com/docs/how-to-ask-properly), [generative-ai page](https://www.revealdata.com/product/generative-ai)).
- **aji** (announced Aug 5, 2025; live late Sept 2025): Reveal's named GenAI review engine — GenAI-assisted document review with a patent-pending "Calibration" workflow, an "AI Advisor" that refines review definitions, per-document reasoning and citations, and a Hybrid mode blending GenAI with Reveal's supervised learning (TAR). Offered **free to all legal teams through Dec 31, 2025** as an adoption play; post-2025 pricing not disclosed ([Businesswire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20250805582024/en/Meet-aji-Reveal-Names-Its-GenAI-Review-Engine-Unlocks-Full-No-Cost-Access-for-All-Legal-Teams-Through-2025), [Reveal news](https://www.revealdata.com/news/meet-aji-reveal-names-its-genai-review-platform-unlocks-full-no-cost-access-for-all-legal-teams-through-2025)).
- **AI Model Library**: reusable, LLM-based pre-built AI models transferable across matters ([generative-ai page](https://www.revealdata.com/product/generative-ai)); model count not verified from first-party sources.
- **Agentic AI: NOT FOUND.** Unlike DISCO (agentic Cecilia, Feb 2026 — [LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/02/disco-launches-scaled-agentic-ai-tool-for-large-discovery-and-fact-investigation-matters.html)) and Epiq, I found **no Reveal agentic-AI product announcement** as of July 2026 — a notable gap and possible positioning opening. (Flagged: absence of evidence, not confirmed absence.)

## 3. Entity/relationship analysis (Brainspace) — key section

Brainspace remains Reveal's analytics differentiator: concept clustering ("Cluster Wheel"), transparent concept search, timeline visualization, communication analysis, and entity extraction, dynamically linked views ([ComplexDiscovery buyer's guide](https://complexdiscovery.com/buyers-guide/reveal-brainspace/), [A.I. Solutions comparison](https://automatedintelligentsolutions.com/ediscovery-platform-comparison-disco-review-vs-reveal-brainspace/)). But the current Communications documentation shows this is **email-centric, classic social-network-graph tech**: people/addresses as nodes, edges for message flow, top senders/recipients, domain filters, color-coded communication clusters. Entity resolution is **alias consolidation** ("a collection of different alias email addresses is known as a Person") with **manual node merging** to disambiguate — no probabilistic entity resolution, no cross-source identity graph, and **no knowledge-graph claims found anywhere** ([docs.revealdata.com Brainspace Communications](https://docs.revealdata.com/brainspace/docs/communications), [legacy help](https://brainspace-help.revealdata.com/en/Communications.html)). The docs make no mention of chat/mobile communications inside the network graph. Verdict for your positioning: strong, mature, well-regarded — but architecturally legacy (email-graph era, pre-dating the 2021 merger) with no visible 2025–2026 reinvention of the entity/relationship layer; Reveal's recent R&D energy has gone to GenAI review (aji/Ask), not the graph.

## 4. Evidence types; mobile phone extraction

Documents/email are core. Short-message data (Slack, Teams, SMS, WhatsApp) is supported **via RSMF normalization**: everything is converted into RSMF (an EML wrapper + zipped JSON manifest), grouped into conversations and **sliced into 24-hour windows, each slice becoming a single "document"** for review/export ([docs: Processing Short Message Format (RSMF)](https://docs.revealdata.com/docs/processing-short-message-format-rsmf)). Logikcull subscription tiers include Slack chat filtering ([logikcull.com/pricing](https://www.logikcull.com/pricing)); the Onna acquisition added collection connectors for cloud collaboration tools ([Onna release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20240507774586/en/Reveal-Acquires-Onna-Further-Bolstering-Its-AI-Powered-eDiscovery-Platform-With-Superior-Data-Collection)).

**Cellebrite/UFED: COULD NOT VERIFY native support.** I found no first-party Reveal documentation of direct Cellebrite UFDR/UFED ingestion (searches of docs.revealdata.com and the Logikcull help corpus surfaced nothing; Relativity and Everlaw, by contrast, document UFDR-to-short-message conversion pipelines — [Relativity docs](https://help.relativity.com/RelativityOne/Content/Relativity/Processing/SupportedFileTypes-Cellebrite.htm), [Everlaw guide](https://www.everlaw.com/guides/novel-data-types/handling-mobile-data-in-ediscovery/)). The documented Reveal path is RSMF, implying phone extractions arrive **pre-converted (typically by a service provider) and flattened into 24-hour chat slices** — not phone-data-as-first-class-evidence (no device model, call logs, app artifacts, locations as native objects in any doc I found). Flag: this is inferred from documentation absence plus the RSMF doc; a Reveal SE could have an undocumented UFDR converter.

## 5. Chronology / timeline / narrative

- Brainspace includes a **timeline visualization** linked to its other analytics views ([ComplexDiscovery](https://complexdiscovery.com/buyers-guide/reveal-brainspace/)); Reveal Review 11's dashboard has a document timeline ([DataMine review](https://dataminediscovery.com/reveal-review-11-a-transformative-solution/)).
- Reveal's own GenAI blog states **aji "identifies dates, events, and sequences within documents to assist with timeline construction during investigation"** ([Reveal blog](https://www.revealdata.com/blog/reveals-genai-ediscovery-engine-aji-ask-and-ai-powered-document-review)).
- **Not verified:** any dedicated, citation-backed chronology/narrative-builder product (a "story" workspace with evidence-linked events). NexLP's old "Story Engine" branding did not surface as a live 2025–2026 feature. This looks like a genuine white-space vs. your platform's cited-timeline concept.

## 6. Deployment

Reveal offers **three models: multi-tenant public-cloud SaaS, Reveal Private Deployment (RPD), and hybrid**. In March 2026 it announced a **50% expansion of RPD investment**, citing surging regulated-industry demand; RPD runs on Kubernetes, Windows, or Linux bare-metal (customer cloud or on-prem style), carries the full AI stack (aji, Ask), and Consilio adopted RPD inside its Aurora platform as its premier private-cloud review partner ([Reveal news](https://www.revealdata.com/news/reveal-expands-private-deployment-investment-by-50-as-enterprise-demand-surges), [Businesswire, Mar 10 2026](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260310711462/en/Reveal-Expands-Private-Deployment-Investment-by-50-as-Enterprise-Demand-Surges)). The announcement leans on "legacy on-premise platforms sunsetting by 2028" (unnamed, presumably Relativity Server-class products) — i.e., Reveal is actively marketing private/on-prem-adjacent deployment as a differentiator, a legacy of the IPRO on-prem installed base. So: yes, single-tenant/private options are current and growing, not deprecated.

## 7. Pricing / GTM

- **Channel:** Reveal runs a formal partner program (channel resellers + service providers + technology partners) with exclusive partner pricing, and announced its "largest European investment to date" expanding the partner program across EMEA in July 2026 ([revealdata.com/partner-program](https://www.revealdata.com/partner-program)). The Consilio RPD deal shows large-matter delivery flows heavily through ALSPs/service providers ([RPD release](https://www.revealdata.com/news/reveal-expands-private-deployment-investment-by-50-as-enterprise-demand-surges)). The exact direct-vs-channel revenue split is **not disclosed** (flagged unverified), but the enterprise platform is predominantly partner/service-provider delivered, while direct small-buyer access exists via Logikcull (below).
- **Enterprise pricing:** not published; aji was free through 2025 as a land-grab, post-2025 pricing undisclosed ([aji release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20250805582024/en/Meet-aji-Reveal-Names-Its-GenAI-Review-Engine-Unlocks-Full-No-Cost-Access-for-All-Legal-Teams-Through-2025)).
- **Can a small defense/PI firm buy direct?** Yes — via Logikcull's self-service signup (free trial, no contract) ([logikcull.com/pricing](https://www.logikcull.com/pricing)); the enterprise Reveal platform is quote/partner-driven.

### Logikcull's current status as a distinct product (mid-2026)
Logikcull **remains a distinct, live product and brand** under Reveal — Reveal explicitly kept both platforms as separate offerings by segment ([LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2023/08/in-a-1b-e-discovery-acquisition-double-play-reveal-acquires-both-logikcull-and-ipro.html)), calls itself "provider of the two leading eDiscovery platforms" ([aji release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20250805582024/en/Meet-aji-Reveal-Names-Its-GenAI-Review-Engine-Unlocks-Full-No-Cost-Access-for-All-Legal-Teams-Through-2025)), and is still shipping features to it (Ask GenAI in subscription tiers now; Blackout redaction landing on Logikcull Q1 2026; aji expansion planned) ([logikcull.com/pricing](https://www.logikcull.com/pricing), [Blackout release](https://markets.financialcontent.com/worldnow.ktul/article/bizwire-2025-6-24-blackout-redaction-now-live-on-reveal-enterprise-platform)). **Pay-as-you-go survives**: monthly per-GB-hosted pricing, no commitment, 10 GB minimum, unlimited users/projects, self-service onboarding; annual subscriptions (pitched at government/corporate) add ASK GenAI, Slack chat filtering, PII detection/global redactions ([logikcull.com/pricing](https://www.logikcull.com/pricing)). Exact per-GB rate is no longer printed on the pricing page; third-party trackers cite ~$40/GB/month ([TrustRadius](https://www.trustradius.com/products/logikcull/pricing) — unverified against first-party), and a reseller channel exists ("Logikcull by Premier" selling PAYG — [logikcullbypremier.com](https://logikcullbypremier.com/store/p/logikcull-basic)). One caution flag: post-acquisition user reviews allege billing-policy changes unfavorable to customers ([TrustRadius](https://www.trustradius.com/products/logikcull/pricing) — anecdotal, unverified). Net: the small-firm, buy-direct, PAYG motion your startup might compete with **still exists**, though feature investment now trails the enterprise platform (Blackout/aji arrive on Logikcull only after Reveal Enterprise).

## 8. Defense-side vs plaintiff/prosecution usage

**Largely not discoverable from public sources — flagged.** What is verifiable: Reveal sells to "law firms, corporations, government agencies, and intelligence services" ([aji release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20250805582024/en/Meet-aji-Reveal-Names-Its-GenAI-Review-Engine-Unlocks-Full-No-Cost-Access-for-All-Legal-Teams-Through-2025)); the Consilio/RPD motion targets Fortune 500 and Am Law 200 (predominantly corporate/defense-side work) ([RPD release](https://www.revealdata.com/news/reveal-expands-private-deployment-investment-by-50-as-enterprise-demand-surges)); Brainspace is positioned for investigations, antitrust, and compliance ([A.I. Solutions](https://automatedintelligentsolutions.com/ediscovery-platform-comparison-disco-review-vs-reveal-brainspace/)); Logikcull targets solo/small firms and government (both sides of the v) ([logikcull.com/pricing](https://www.logikcull.com/pricing)). I found no published customer-mix breakdown by litigation side and no criminal-defense/prosecution-specific positioning — notably, **no evidence Reveal is marketed for criminal-defense phone-evidence work at all**, which is your wedge.

## 9. Notable 2025–2026 news

- **Jun 24, 2025** — Blackout redaction (Milyli source code, acquired late 2024) live on Reveal Enterprise at no extra cost; Logikcull rollout Q1 2026 ([release](https://markets.financialcontent.com/worldnow.ktul/article/bizwire-2025-6-24-blackout-redaction-now-live-on-reveal-enterprise-platform)).
- **Aug 5 / late Sep 2025** — aji GenAI review engine named and launched; free for all legal teams through 2025 ([Businesswire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20250805582024/en/Meet-aji-Reveal-Names-Its-GenAI-Review-Engine-Unlocks-Full-No-Cost-Access-for-All-Legal-Teams-Through-2025)).
- **Mar 10, 2026** — RPD private-deployment investment +50%; Consilio Aurora names Reveal premier private-cloud review partner ([Businesswire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260310711462/en/Reveal-Expands-Private-Deployment-Investment-by-50-as-Enterprise-Demand-Surges)).
- **Jul 2026** — largest-ever European investment; partner program expanded across EMEA ([partner-program](https://www.revealdata.com/partner-program)).
- Context: 2026 eDiscovery M&A has slowed and shifted to AI-capability tuck-ins; no new Reveal transactions recorded through July 13, 2026 ([ComplexDiscovery](https://complexdiscovery.com/ediscovery-ma-tracker-update-nine-deals-and-two-themes/)). Competitors' agentic launches (DISCO Feb 2026 — [LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/02/disco-launches-scaled-agentic-ai-tool-for-large-discovery-and-fact-investigation-matters.html); Epiq — [epiqglobal.com](https://www.epiqglobal.com/en-us/resource-center/news/epiq-announces-expanded-agentic-ai-offerings-for-legal-and-compliance)) have no announced Reveal counterpart.

### Explicitly unverified / gaps
1. Native Cellebrite UFDR/UFED ingestion (no first-party doc found; RSMF conversion is the documented path). 2. Any Reveal agentic-AI roadmap. 3. AI Model Library size/current model count. 4. Post-2025 aji pricing. 5. Current first-party Logikcull per-GB rate. 6. Direct-vs-channel revenue split. 7. Defense/plaintiff customer mix. 8. Any 2025–2026 funding/valuation event. 9. A dedicated citation-backed chronology/narrative product (beyond aji's date/event extraction and Brainspace's timeline view).

**Key competitive takeaways for an AI-native investigative evidence platform:** Reveal is the scale incumbent with strong GenAI review (aji) and precision-only Q&A (Ask, top-100-source RAG with explicit recall disclaimers), but its entity/relationship layer is legacy email-graph Brainspace with manual node merging and no knowledge graph; mobile evidence is flattened into RSMF 24-hour slices rather than first-class phone data; there is no cited-chronology/narrative product and no agentic story; and small-firm direct access exists only through the maintained-but-second-priority Logikcull.
---
## 4.5 Casepoint

# Casepoint Competitive Research Report (as of July 2026)

**Note on scope/verification:** Web-search budget was exhausted mid-task; findings after that point come from direct fetches of casepoint.com and press URLs. Anything I could not verify is explicitly flagged in each section.

---

## 1. One-Paragraph Description

Casepoint is a cloud-native legal data discovery platform (legal hold, eDiscovery, investigations, and FOIA) founded around a litigation-services heritage and now overwhelmingly oriented toward the U.S. federal government market. In **January 2025, Casepoint merged with OPEXUS** (government process-management software: FOIA, audit, investigations workflow) with a **majority investment from Thoma Bravo**; Casepoint shareholders and OPEXUS investor Gemspring Capital retained minority stakes, and the combined entity is headquartered in Washington, D.C. ([eDiscovery Today](https://ediscoverytoday.com/2025/01/27/opexus-and-casepoint-announce-merger-and-majority-investment-from-thoma-bravo/), [Casepoint press](https://www.casepoint.com/press/opexus-casepoint-merger-and-majority-investment-from-thoma-bravo/), [Legal IT Insider](https://legaltechnology.com/2025/01/27/private-equity-firm-thoma-bravo-acquires-and-merges-casepoint-and-opexus/)). Scale markers: OPEXUS brought 100,000+ government users across 200+ public institutions; Casepoint serves dozens of federal agencies plus enterprises, e.g., the SEC runs 4,200 users / 800 TB / 2,800 active matters on it ([Casepoint SEC press release](https://www.casepoint.com/press/casepoint-achieves-fedramp-authorization-marketwatch/)). Leadership churned post-merger: long-time Casepoint CEO Haresh Bhungalia departed at the acquisition, OPEXUS CEO Howard Langsam led until ~September 2025, an interim co-CEO period followed (CMO Krystal Putman-Garcia was "Co-interim CEO" in March 2026), and **GovTech veteran Paul Colangelo was named CEO in June 2026** ([LawSites](https://www.lawnext.com/2026/06/casepoint-names-govtech-veteran-paul-colangelo-ceo-as-thoma-bravo-backed-company-pushes-deeper-into-government.html), [eDiscovery Today](https://ediscoverytoday.com/2026/06/08/casepoint-appoints-paul-colangelo-as-chief-executive-officer/)). Biggest recent news: a sole-award **up-to-$98.8M Pentagon blanket purchase agreement (June 2026)** for AI-powered eDiscovery across DOD OGC/DISA/DLSA ([DefenseScoop](https://defensescoop.com/2026/06/24/pentagon-casepoint-ai-products-enhance-classified-legal-ops/)).

**Flag — "Aurora":** I found **no evidence of any Casepoint product or codename "Aurora"** in official pages or legal-tech press. The only "Aurora" AI platform surfacing in searches is Arctic Wolf's (cybersecurity, unrelated) ([Arctic Wolf](https://arcticwolf.com/resources/press-releases/arctic-wolf-unveils-the-aurora-superintelligence-platform-to-redefine-security-for-the-age-of-ai/)). Casepoint's AI brands are **CasepointAI / "Casepoint AI"** and **CaseAssist**. Treat "Aurora" as unverified / likely a mistaken attribution.

## 2. Generative-AI Capabilities 2025–2026

Marketed as "Casepoint AI" (historically the CasepointAI suite) layered on the older **CaseAssist** TAR/active-learning engine ([Casepoint gen-AI page](https://www.casepoint.com/generative-ai-solution/), [CaseAssist PR, 2021](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/casepoint-unveils-latest-iteration-of-ai-and-advanced-analytics-technology-caseassist-301313356.html)):

- **Chat-based natural-language Q&A over case data** with "evidence-based answers in seconds"; **document summarization** (contracts, lengthy docs); **auto-classification** of mixed document types ([gen-AI page](https://www.casepoint.com/generative-ai-solution/), [eDiscovery software page](https://www.casepoint.com/solutions/ediscovery-software/)).
